Result: 逐传感器令牌化配合交叉注意力在所有基准-训练组合中使经典DeepONet的平均相对L2误差降低2.4-28.0倍，最佳注意力配置可达到3.5-32.3倍。分支自注意力仅与点积融合搭配时表现不一致（退化一维问题但改善二维源场问题）；叠加在交叉注意力之上可在全部六个案例中提升精度，但改善幅度小于单独使用交叉注意力融合。全局预混合无一致收益。增加交叉注意力深度进一步提升精度，但收益递减，且在物理信息训练下代价显著增大。

Conclusion: 查询依赖的交叉注意力是提升DeepONet精度最可靠的注意力机制；分支自注意力仅对大型、空间复杂的函数输入最具价值。研究为神经算子中注意力机制的设计选择提供了清晰的实证指导。

Abstract: Deep neural operators learn mappings between input functions and complete PDE solution fields, enabling forward evaluations of new problem instances orders of magnitude faster than conventional numerical solvers. Attention mechanisms have recently been introduced into neural operators, but most studies change several architectural components at once, making it difficult to identify what actually improves accuracy. This work presents a controlled and systematic study of five deep operator network (DeepONet) variants with distinct attention mechanisms, trained under both data-driven and physics-informed regimes, to isolate the effects of cross-attention, self-attention, tokenization, and attention depth. We evaluate them on a source-driven transient one-dimensional nonlinear diffusion-reaction equation, a transient one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation with variable initial conditions, and a two-dimensional Poisson heat-conduction problem with heterogeneous source fields. Per-sensor tokenization with cross-attention reduces the mean relative L_2 error of the classical DeepONet in all benchmark-training combinations by factors of 2.4-28.0, while the best attention configurations reach 3.5-32.3. Branch self-attention paired only with dot-product fusion is inconsistent, degrading the one-dimensional problems while helping the more complex two-dimensional source field; added on top of cross-attention it improves all six cases, though by less than cross-attention fusion alone. Global pre-mixing provides no consistent benefit. Increasing cross-attention depth further improves accuracy, but with diminishing returns and a substantially higher cost under physics-informed training. Overall, query-dependent cross-attention is the most reliable mechanism, whereas branch self-attention is most useful for large, spatially complex functional inputs.

</details>


### [264] [REFINE: LLM Refinement over Budgeted Text-Attributed Graphs for Personalized Medical Concept Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04415)
*Mohsen Nayebi Kerdabadi,Arya Hadizadeh Moghaddam,Dongjie Wang,Zijun Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REFINE，一种基于知识图谱感知的预算化LLM图细化框架，用于患者个性化的医学概念编码。通过强化学习为每个观察代码选择个性化KG扩展预算，由异构GNN捕获结构依赖，并用冻结LLM通过图感知软提示进行语义细化，在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上显著提升多个EHR骨干模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本属性知识图谱(TKG)为EHR预测提供结构化和文本基础，但现有编码器对所有患者统一处理概念，忽略了医学代码的含义和预测价值依赖于患者特定临床情境和轨迹的事实。现有方法面临两大挑战：(1)如何为每个观察代码决定纳入多少KG上下文；(2)如何将语义信息与患者特定的关系结构对齐。

Method: REFINE框架包含三个核心组件：(1)从全局TKG构建患者特定的时间图谱；(2)采用顺序强化学习策略为每个观察代码选择和分配个性化的KG扩展预算，实现自适应的上下文规模控制；(3)使用异构GNN处理患者图以捕获关系感知的结构依赖性，同时利用冻结LLM通过图感知软提示对概念表示进行语义细化。

Result: 在MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV上的实验表明，REFINE一致性地提升了多种EHR骨干模型的性能，优于强基线方法，并在组件消融、KG选择和低数据充足性条件下表现出稳健的性能增益。

Conclusion: REFINE通过引入预算控制的KG扩展和LLM语义细化机制，有效实现了患者个性化的医学概念表示学习，为利用TKG和LLM提升EHR预测提供了可行的框架方案。

Abstract: Learning rich medical concept representations is essential for EHR prediction. Text-attributed knowledge graphs (TKGs) provide a natural foundation by organizing heterogeneous medical relations together with textual semantics. However, most existing encoders process concepts uniformly across patients, despite the fact that a code's meaning and predictive value depend on patient-specific clinical context and trajectory. Learning patient-personalized concept representations from TKGs introduces two key challenges: (1) deciding how much KG context to incorporate for each observed code, and (2) aligning semantic information with the patient-specific relational structure. We propose REFINE, a KG-aware budgeted LLM graph refinement framework for patient-personalized medical concept encoding. Starting from a global TKG, REFINE constructs patient-specific temporal graphs. A sequential reinforcement learning policy selects a personalized KG expansion budget for each observed code. The resulting patient graph is processed by a heterogeneous GNN to capture relation-aware structural dependencies, while a frozen LLM uses graph-aware soft prompts to semantically refine concept representations. Experiments on MIMIC-III and MIMIC-IV show that REFINE consistently improves diverse EHR backbones, outperforms strong baselines, and demonstrates robust gains across component ablation, KG selection, and data insufficiency.

</details>


### [265] [Beyond a Universal Forecasting Selector: Demand-Conditioned Model Selection across Demand Patterns and Horizons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04425)
*Adolfo González*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了在异构需求环境下，预测模型选择器（selector）本身是否应被视为取决于上下文（context-dependent）的组件。通过在24个优化预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分及1-12周期范围内，比较RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD五种选择机制，发现没有任何选择器在所有条件下占优：CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD更适用于平滑需求，而OWA和ERA在间歇性和批量性需求中表现更佳；且选择器的适用性随数据可用性和预测周期变化而变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异构需求条件下，预测模型选择仍然困难，因为最合适的决策规则可能随需求结构、数据可用性和预测周期而变化。本文旨在检验选择器本身是否应被视为预测过程中一个依赖上下文的组件，而非普适的固定规则。

Method: 研究采用五种选择机制（RMSSE、ERA、OWA、CCG-AHSC、CCG-AHSCD），在24个优化的预测模型、9个数据集、3种训练-测试划分以及1至12周期的条件下进行比较。选择器性能通过事后（ex post）全局相对精度（GRA）、统计检验以及最佳可实现模型参考基准进行评估。

Result: 没有任何一种选择器在所有条件下占据主导地位。CCG-AHSC和CCG-AHSCD在平滑需求及若干Erratic配置下更具竞争力，而OWA和ERA在间歇性（Intermittent）和批量性（Lumpy）需求设定中表现更好。选择器的适用性还随历史数据可用性和预测周期的变化而改变。

Conclusion: 选择器的适宜性取决于具体上下文，研究结果支持采用依赖上下文的（context-dependent）预测模型选择方法，而非普适性（universal）选择策略。

Abstract: Forecasting-model selection remains difficult in heterogeneous demand because the most suitable decision rule may vary with demand structure, data availability, and forecasting horizon. This study examines whether the selector itself should be treated as a context-dependent component of the forecasting process. Five selection mechanisms - RMSSE, ERA, OWA, CCG-AHSC, and CCG-AHSCD - are compared across 24 optimized forecasting models, nine datasets, three training-testing partitions, and horizons from 1 to 12 cycles. Selector performance is evaluated ex post using Global Relative Accuracy (GRA), statistical tests, and a best-attainable-model reference. No selector dominates across all conditions. CCG-AHSC and CCG-AHSCD are more competitive for Smooth demand and several Erratic configurations, whereas OWA and ERA perform better in Intermittent and Lumpy settings. Selector suitability also changes with historical data availability and horizon, supporting a context-dependent rather than universal approach to forecasting-model selection.

</details>


### [266] [A Repeated-Measurement Study for Cultural Analytics of English Song Lyrics Using Five Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04428)
*E. Cho Smith,Samuel Ho,Dawn Laux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了五种大语言模型（LLMs）作为零样本标注器，对英文歌词中四种社会构念（自尊、自我控制、归属寻求、认同寻求）进行标注的可靠性，并发现LLM测量并非在所有构念上均一致可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被越来越多地用于大规模文化文本标注，其输出被视为潜在社会构念的测量值之前，必须验证这些测量的可靠性，但目前缺乏系统的评估。

Method: 使用五种LLM对大规模歌词语料进行重复零样本标注，考察三个维度：重复运行的一致性、跨模型收敛性、以及共识标签对监督分类的可迁移性。

Result: LLM测量并非在所有构念上可靠：自尊的重复测量信度最强，认同寻求最不稳定，自我控制和归属寻求居中但依赖具体模型。下游分类表明共识LLM标签包含可学习的信号，但可迁移性本身不能确立构念效度。

Conclusion: 在LLM标注被当作文化分析的可扩展测量之前，应报告重复测量稳定性和跨模型收敛性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to annotate cultural texts at scales that are impractical for human coders. However, before their outputs are treated as measurements of latent social constructs, it is necessary to establish whether those measurements are reliable. This study evaluates five LLMs as zero-shot annotators of four social constructs expressed in English song lyrics: self-esteem, self-control, seeking belonging, and seeking recognition. Using repeated annotations of a large lyric corpus, we examine three properties of LLM-based measurement: consistency across repeated runs, convergence across models, and transferability of consensus labels to supervised classification. The findings show that LLM-based measurement is not uniformly reliable across constructs. Self-esteem exhibits the strongest repeated-measurement reliability across models, while seeking recognition is generally less stable; self-control and seeking belonging show intermediate but model-dependent reliability. Downstream classification further indicates that consensus LLM labels contain learnable signal, although transferability does not itself establish construct validity. Repeated-measurement stability and cross-model convergence should therefore be reported before LLM annotations are treated as scalable measurements in cultural analytics.

</details>


### [267] [Conformity Breaks Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04445)
*Yibo Hu,Hanyu Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现多智能体LLM系统中，一致性证书在模型单独回答时有效，但当模型看到一致错误答案的同伴时失效，导致校准覆盖准确率从90%骤降至74%，且在低置信度子组上从87%降至47%，标准conformal方法无法修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有conformal预测方法假设模型评分行为在部署环境中保持一致，但多智能体LLM系统中同伴压力会改变模型的评分机制，导致基于干净环境的校准在实际部署中失效，可能带来安全隐患。

Method: 提出"评分机制偏移"（score-mechanism shift）概念，通过在开放权重模型和多项选择QA任务上，比较LLM在单独回答与看到一致错误答案同伴时的conformal覆盖率差异，并分析低置信度子组上的恶意攻击效应。

Result: 校准的90%覆盖率在一致错误同伴环境下降至74%（alpha=0.10）；攻击者针对低置信度子组可使覆盖率从87%近乎减半至47%；标准conformal修复无法解决问题，因为问题分布未变，变化的是模型评分行为。

Conclusion: 多智能体LLM系统中的peer pressure会改变模型评分机制，使conformal预测的校准失效，且现有修复手段无效，需要开发对评分机制偏移鲁棒的新方法。

Abstract: A conformal certificate can be valid when an LLM answers alone and invalid when the same LLM sees peers that unanimously assert a wrong answer. The question is unchanged; the model's score for the correct answer changes. We call this a score-mechanism shift: clean calibration certifies how the model scores answers alone, but not how it scores them under peer pressure. We show that this shift silently breaks conformal prediction in multi-agent LLM systems. Across open-weight models and multiple-choice QA tasks, coverage falls from a calibrated 90% to 74% under unanimous-wrong peers at the standard alpha = 0.10 operating point. The average hides a sharper failure: by targeting the low-confidence items the certificate still covers, an attacker nearly halves coverage on that subgroup, from 87% to 47%, while the monitored average remains much higher. The failure also reaches the decision layer: a system that should escalate when uncertain can instead become confident enough to act on the attacker's wrong answer. Standard conformal fixes do not solve the problem, because the question distribution has not changed; the model's scoring behavior has.

</details>


### [268] [When Load-Balancing Goes Too Far: Expert Pruning in Over-Dispersed Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04453)
*Berkcan Kapusuzoglu,Connor Pryor,Sangwoo Cho,Supriyo Chakraborty,Shi-Xiong Zhang,Sambit Sahu,Milind Naphade*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究MoE模型在过度分散路由（over-dispersed routing）下的专家剪枝问题，指出当路由器概率因激进的负载均衡而近似均匀分布时，基于路由器概率的重要性信号失效，困惑度无法预测下游任务准确率。作者提出MESA（Minimax Expert Score Allocation）方法，通过领域感知的迭代式重要性评分分配来最小化最坏情况的领域性能退化，在25%专家剪枝时优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoE专家剪枝方法假设路由器概率能可靠地指示专家重要性，但这一假设在过度分散路由下（由训练过程中激进的负载均衡导致）失效。此时路由器将token近乎均匀地分配给各专家，重要性信号坍缩，导致困惑度与下游任务准确率脱钩，甚至出现低困惑度配置反而损害数学推理能力的现象。这暴露了现有剪枝范式在负载均衡MoE模型上的根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA)，一种领域感知的迭代式评分分配方法。其核心思想是：在每次迭代中识别当前受影响最严重的领域，并针对性地提升服务该领域的专家的重要性分数，从而最小化最坏情况下的领域退化而非平均准确率。该方法在设计上明确区分了过度分散路由与标准路由两种不同剪枝区间。

Result: 在25%专家剪枝设置下，MESA实现了最小的跨领域最坏情况退化，在11个基准中优于激活感知基线7个；该方法泛化到gpt-oss-120B、Gemma-4-26B-A4B和OLMoE-1B-7B等不同规模的模型。同时揭示了一个关键现象：过度分散路由下的剪枝存在能力权衡——激活感知评分保留数学推理却严重损害知识密集型科学任务（GPQA上18分差距），而频率评分则呈现相反模式。

Conclusion: 过度分散路由是一个与标准路由本质上不同的剪枝区间，标准假设（路由器概率作为重要性信号、困惑度作为质量代理）在该区间失效。认识并区分这一区间是对负载均衡MoE模型进行原则性专家剪枝的前提条件；MESA作为领域感知的防御性方法，为这类模型的剪枝提供了更稳健的策略。

Abstract: Expert pruning reduces the memory and serving cost of Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models by removing low-importance experts identified by the router, assuming router probabilities provide a reliable importance signal. We observe that this assumption breaks down under over-dispersed routing, a regime associated with aggressive load-balancing during training, in which tokens are distributed nearly uniformly across experts and importance signals collapse. In this regime, perplexity does not predict downstream task accuracy: on gpt-oss-20B, the lowest-perplexity pruning configuration yields the worst mathematical reasoning, while the highest-perplexity configuration preserves it. This does not occur under standard routing (e.g., Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct), where perplexity and accuracy degrade together. Pruning under over-dispersed routing also exposes a capability trade-off in which no single scoring metric dominates: activation-aware scoring preserves mathematical reasoning but severely degrades knowledge-intensive science (an 18-point gap on GPQA), whereas frequency-based scoring exhibits the reverse. We propose Minimax Expert Score Allocation (MESA), a domain-aware method that iteratively boosts importance scores for experts serving whichever domain is currently worst-affected, minimizing worst-case domain degradation rather than average accuracy. At 25% expert pruning MESA achieves the smallest worst-case degradation across domains, outperforming activation-aware baselines on 7 of 11 benchmarks at a correspondingly reduced memory footprint, and it generalizes to gpt-oss-120B, Gemma-4-26B-A4B, and OLMoE-1B-7B. Our results indicate that over-dispersed routing is a qualitatively distinct pruning regime in which standard assumptions fail, and that recognizing it is a prerequisite for principled expert pruning of load-balanced MoE models.

</details>


### [269] [On-board ML for Trace Gas detection in Imaging Spectroscopy data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04458)
*Vít Růžička,Adam Chlus,Andrew Thorpe,David R. Thompson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文报道了在2026年东京野外试验中，首次利用机载边缘机器学习（Edge ML）在AVIRIS-5成像光谱仪上实时检测甲烷点源排放，通过只下行传输潜在事件而非完整数据立方体，克服了通信带宽瓶颈，缩短了从探测到信息获取的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中和星载成像光谱数据虽能探测痕量气体排放等瞬态事件，但现有处理流程依赖缓慢的地面处理，导致事件信息延迟，无法进行即时后续行动。

Method: 在2026年3月东京野外试验期间，在搭载AVIRIS-5传感器的平台上实施机载数据处理方案；由于通信瓶颈无法即时下行完整数据立方体，采用高效且规模小（small）的机器学习模型在机预测潜在事件，仅下行传输预测结果。

Result: 首次实现了利用成像光谱数据进行甲烷点源排放的机载（on-board）实时探测（Edge ML），验证了边缘推理在气体排放检测中的可行性。

Conclusion: 机载Edge ML可通过下行仅传输事件预测结果的方式显著提升探测时效，为未来瞬态气体事件的即时响应提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Data collected during aerial and spaceborne imaging spectroscopy campaigns enables the detection of transient events such as trace gas emissions. However, current processing pipelines depend on slow, on-the-ground processing, which delays the time to information of each detected event and prohibits immediate follow-up actions. During the Tokyo Field Campaign of March 2026, we explored on-board processing of Imaging Spectroscopy data from the equipped AVIRIS-5 sensor. Due to communication bottlenecks, full datacubes cannot be downlinked immediately during the flight. Instead we downlink the potential events predicted by our efficient and small machine learning model. We show the first on-board detection of methane point source emission with Imaging Spectroscopy data using Edge ML.

</details>


### [270] [Nested Inductive Bias Framework for SPD Manifold Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04466)
*Tushar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Nested Inductive Bias框架，通过两阶段微分同胚合成将非欧几里得目标几何拉回到SPD流形上，同时满足流形约束与关系先验，并设计了Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)以提升向量化架构的几何鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SPD流形表示学习多依赖Log-Euclidean等pullback欧氏度量来满足流形约束，虽然计算高效且避免边界违规，但其平坦几何无法捕获数据集内在的关系先验（relational priors）。Poincaré等度量虽广泛用于诱导领域对齐的关系先验，但将其从标准向量表示推广到SPD流形一直存在挑战。因此需要弥合这一差距。

Method: 提出Nested Inductive Bias框架，采用两阶段微分同胚（two-stage diffeomorphic）合成，将非欧几里得目标几何正式pull back到SPD流形上。该框架允许构造曲率对齐的黎曼分类器，同时尊重矩阵约束和数据潜在关系几何。此外，对于标准向量化架构，提出Rational Conformal Metric (RCM)，通过有界表示空间来建立针对异常值的最先进几何鲁棒性。

Result: 在运动学与信号处理基准以及合成实验上的实证评估表明：除非度量曲率与内在数据分布对齐，否则深度流形网络会出现类别可分性退化。RCM在标准向量化架构上展现了针对异常值的最先进几何鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 度量曲率与数据内在分布的匹配对于深度流形网络的性能至关重要。Nested Inductive Bias框架成功将非欧几里得目标几何推广到SPD域，实现了同时尊重矩阵约束和潜在关系几何的曲率对齐分类器；RCM则为向量化架构提供了更强的几何鲁棒性。

Abstract: In Geometric Deep Learning, inductive biases serve two primary functions: enforcing manifold constraints and embedding relational priors. Currently, representation learning on SPD manifolds frequently relies on pullback Euclidean metrics, such as the Log-Euclidean Metric, to satisfy the former. While computationally efficient in avoiding domain boundary violations, these metrics induce a flat geometry that may fail to capture the intrinsic relational priors of datasets. While metrics such as the Poincaré metric are widely utilized to induce domain-aligned relational priors, generalizing them from standard vector representations to the SPD manifold has remained a challenge. To bridge this gap, we introduce a Nested Inductive Bias framework that utilizes a two-stage diffeomorphic composition to formally pull back non-Euclidean target geometries onto the SPD manifold. This framework enables the construction of curvature-aligned Riemannian classifiers that simultaneously respect matrix constraints and the latent relational geometry of the data. Empirical evaluations on kinematic and signal processing benchmarks, together with synthetic experiments, demonstrate that deep manifold networks experience degradation in class separability unless the metric curvature aligns with the intrinsic data distribution. Furthermore, for standard vectorized architectures, we propose the Rational Conformal Metric (RCM), designed to establish state-of-the-art geometric robustness against outliers by bounding the representation space.

</details>


### [271] [Hakken: Predicting future discoveries to fill the gaps in today's knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04494)
*Tarek R. Besold,Uchenna Akujuobi,Pablo Sanchez,Alessandra Toniato,Kana Maruyama,Jihun Choi,Samy Badreddine,Frederick Gifford,Daniel Evans-Yamamoto,Sucheendra K. Palaniappan,Miquel Ferrer,Kae Nagano,Iris Rossell,Tom Joy,Hatem ElShazly,Chrysa Iliopoulou,Christoph Wehner,Thiviyan Thanapalasingam,Susana Nunes,Pedro G. Cotovio,Peter Wurman,Peter Stone,Hiroaki Kitano,Michael Spranger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Hakken系统，一个跨领域的知识预测与解释系统，利用基于Transformer的预测模型（结合时间序列知识图谱与LLM语义知识）预测未记录的科学概念间关系，并通过模型无关解释框架为预测提供可评价信息。在生物医学领域，Hakken建立时间感知多标签关系预测的新基准，从150万条超过置信阈值的老龄化相关假说中推进三条进行湿实验验证，其中两条预测（TP53-BAMBI和RAF1-TNF相互作用）已获实验确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有科学知识发现系统受限于已有知识的演绎推理，难以发现超越演绎封闭的全新关系。研究者需要一种能够预测"尚未被记录"的科学概念间关系的系统，以加速科学知识增长，特别是在生物医学等复杂领域。

Method: Hakken系统包含两大核心组件：(1) 基于Transformer的预测模型，构建于从大量研究文献中提取的时间序列知识图谱之上，并与LLM的语义知识融合，用于预测未记录关系是否存在及其类型；(2) 模型无关（model-agnostic）的解释框架，为每个预测提供辅助信息，帮助科学家评估建议的新关系。

Result: 在生物医学领域，Hakken的预测模型在时间感知多标签关系预测任务上建立新基准；模型输出在历史数据的长时间段内保持连贯和信息量。系统生成了150万条与老龄化相关的超阈值假说，经生物学家定性验证后，三条约进湿实验验证，其中TP53-BAMBI和RAF1-TNF两条此前未知的相互作用被实验确认，具有药物发现与老药新用的潜在重大影响。

Conclusion: Hakken证明了领域无关的知识预测系统能够超越已有知识边界，通过结合时序知识图谱与LLM语义能力，有效预测并验证新颖的科学关系，展示了AI驱动的科学发现在实际研究中的可行性与重大价值。

Abstract: We present Hakken, a domain-agnostic prediction and explanation system performing knowledge prediction, i.e., growing scientific knowledge by establishing novel relationships, ones that are not limited to the deductive hull of previous knowledge. Hakken uses a transformer-based prediction model built on temporal sequences of knowledge graphs extracted from vast bodies of research publications, fused with an LLM's semantic knowledge, to predict the presence and define the type of as-yet undocumented relationships between scientific concepts. It then calls a model-agnostic explanation framework to provide accompanying information for each prediction that allows scientists to evaluate the suggested new relationship. While general purpose, we demonstrate Hakken's practical capabilities by applying it to the biomedical domain. There, Hakken's prediction model establishes a new benchmark for time-aware multi-label relation prediction, and we show that the model's output stays coherent and informative over extended time spans in historic data. In addition, we scored 1.5 million above-confidence-threshold hypotheses related to aging, qualitatively validated batches of these predictions with biologists and progressed three of them for empirical validation in wet-lab. Two predictions with potentially significant impact in the context of drug discovery and repurposing were confirmed, introducing previously undocumented interactions between TP53 and BAMBI, and between RAF1 and TNF, to biomedical science.

</details>


### [272] [An Energy-Based Conservative-Dissipative Latent Neural Evolution Operator for Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04530)
*Sebastian Schaffer,Lukas Exl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于能量的磁化动力学降阶模型，将卷积自编码器与结构化隐空间神经常微分方程相结合，利用Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程的进动-耗散结构，通过反对称算子和对称半正定耗散算子从学习到的标量势能梯度生成隐向量场，在短轨迹窗口上联合训练，实现比微磁求解器更廉价的轨迹预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微磁求解器计算成本高，难以直接用于长时间、多样本场景下的磁化动力学仿真；同时，现有的数据驱动降阶模型往往忽略LLG方程的物理结构，需要时间导数监督或能量标签等额外信息，缺乏物理约束的同时难以保证长时间预测的稳定性与精度。

Method: 该方法将卷积自编码器与结构化隐空间神经常微分方程耦合：编码器将磁化状态映射到低维隐空间，隐空间中的向量场由学习到的标量势能梯度通过反对称算子（模拟进动）和对称半正定耗散算子（模拟耗散）生成。编码器、解码器、隐能量及算子通过网络联合训练，损失仅为潜在轨迹损失和重解码轨迹损失，无需时间导数监督、物理能量标签或耗散惩罚项。推理时仅需编码一次初始状态，在隐空间中演化，再按需解码输出时刻。实验比较了二次、深度及加性深度-二次三种隐能量形式，在NIST μMAG标准问题4的两个外加磁场方向数据集上评估。

Result: 在短的训练风格窗口上，仅耗散模型和反对称-耗散模型精度相当，但在不间断的长rollout中，反对称-耗散模型提供显著更精确的轨迹预测。深度-二次隐能量在两个磁场方向上都给出最佳精度，且当rollout扩展到训练时域的两倍时误差增长更慢。

Conclusion: 利用LLG方程的进动-耗散结构构建的能量基隐空间模型，能够在无需额外物理监督的情况下实现准确的长时间磁化动力学预测；反对称分量的引入对长时间轨迹预测至关重要，深层隐能量表示优于简单的二次能量，所提出的降阶方法在推理效率上远优于微磁求解器。

Abstract: We develop an energy-based reduced-order model for micromagnetic magnetization dynamics that couples a convolutional autoencoder to a structured latent neural ordinary differential equation. Motivated by the precessional-dissipative structure of the Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert equation, the latent vector field is generated from the gradient of a learned scalar potential through an antisymmetric operator and a symmetric positive-semidefinite dissipative operator. This potential is learned in nonunique latent coordinates and is not identified with the Gibbs free energy, but decreases monotonically along autonomous continuous-time solutions, while the antisymmetric component permits motion along its level sets. The encoder, decoder, latent energy, and operators are trained jointly on short trajectory windows using latent and decoded-rollout losses alone, without time-derivative supervision, physical-energy labels, or dissipation penalties. At inference, an initial state is encoded once, evolved in latent space, and decoded only at the requested output times, enabling substantially cheaper trajectory prediction than the micromagnetic solver used to generate the training data. We compare quadratic, deep, and additive deep-quadratic latent energies on two datasets parameterized by field amplitude and generated for the two applied-field directions of the NIST $μ$MAG Standard Problem 4. Dissipative-only and antisymmetric-dissipative models achieve comparable accuracy on short training-style windows but differ substantially on uninterrupted rollouts, for which the antisymmetric-dissipative models provide markedly more accurate trajectory predictions. The deep-quadratic energy gives the best overall accuracy for both field directions and exhibits slower error growth when rollouts are extended to twice the training horizon.

</details>


### [273] [Distilled Continuous Diffusion Language Models Can Write Code in Few Steps---or One](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04531)
*Fred Zhangzhi Peng,Kaiwen Zheng,Anru R. Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PlaidQ，一个0.7B参数的连续扩散语言模型，用于代码生成。通过两种蒸馏策略（分布匹配用于少步生成，成对轨迹监督用于单步生成），将扩散模型的去噪轨迹压缩至极少数步甚至一步，在代码生成任务上取得竞争力的性能，建立了一条连续扩散模型实现高效少步/单步生成的可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成几乎普遍被视为序列过程：自回归模型逐token输出，而扩散语言模型则以长迭代细化轨迹取代token级序列性。作者的动机在于弥合这两种范式，探索连续扩散是否可以作为语言生成的一般接口，使模型能够继承连续扩散建模中的加速和蒸馏机制，从而突破当前扩散语言模型迭代步数多、效率低的瓶颈。

Method: PlaidQ将预训练的自回归模型重新用作连续token嵌入上的双向去噪器。蒸馏采用两种策略：分布匹配用于少步生成，成对轨迹监督用于一步生成，将扩散模型的长轨迹压缩到几步甚至一步。

Result: 在同等模型规模下，PlaidQ与离散扩散语言模型在代码生成任务上具有竞争力。经蒸馏后质量-计算前沿被显著推移：16步学生模型在HumanEval和MBPP+上分别达到31.78和40.49的pass@10，超越512步采样的教师模型。极端情况下，成对轨迹蒸馏以单步去噪在HumanEval上达到7.07 pass@1，能生成功能正确的程序。

Conclusion: 连续扩散不仅只是语言的另一种表征方式，它提供了一种接口，使语言模型能够继承连续扩散建模的加速和蒸馏机制。实验结果确立了连续扩散作为少步和单步代码生成可行路径的地位，并在代码库中提供了训练和推理的实现。

Abstract: Language generation is almost universally treated as a sequential process: autoregressive models emit one token at a time, while diffusion language models replace token-level seriality with a long trajectory of iterative refinement. In this work, we introduce PlaidQ, a 0.7B continuous diffusion language model for code generation, and show that its trajectory can be aggressively distilled into only a few denoising steps---or even one, enabling efficient code generation. PlaidQ repurposes a pretrained autoregressive model as a bidirectional denoiser over continuous token embeddings. We distill PlaidQ with distribution matching for few-step generation and paired-trajectory supervision for one-step generation. At matched model scale, PlaidQ is competitive with discrete diffusion language models on code generation. Distillation then shifts the quality--compute frontier: a 16-step student reaches 31.78 and 40.49 pass@10 on HumanEval and MBPP+, surpassing the same PlaidQ teacher sampled for 512 steps. At the extreme, paired-trajectory distillation achieves 7.07 pass@1 on HumanEval with a single denoising step, producing functionally correct programs. Together, these results establish continuous diffusion as a viable path to few-step and one-step code generation. Broadly, continuous diffusion is not merely another representation for language: it provides an interface through which language models can inherit the acceleration and distillation machinery of continuous diffusion modeling. Training and inference code and model checkpoints are available at https://github.com/pengzhangzhi/plaidq.

</details>


### [274] [Mitra-v2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04540)
*Yefan Tao,Xiyuan Zhang,Xinyi Liu,Boran Han,Danielle Maddix,Haoyang Fang,Zhen Han,Jiading Gai,Xuanqing Liu,Michael Bohlke-Schneider,Yuyang,Wang,Gerald Friedland,Kevan Mah,Chris Lee,Chris Kong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mitra-v2 是一个仅使用合成数据训练的表格基础模型，基于小型 2D Transformer 骨干网络，支持更长上下文和更大特征空间。在 TabArena 和 TALENT 基准（涵盖 300+ 真实数据集）上达到 SOTA 性能，与 1.6B 参数的 TabFM 持平但仅用其 5% 的参数量（77M），并大幅超越 TabPFN-3。模型权重和代码以 Apache-2.0 许可证开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个高效、广泛适用的开源表格基础模型，在分类和回归任务上达到前沿性能，同时通过仅使用合成数据和大规模多样化预训练分布来规避真实数据隐私问题，并以极小参数量实现与大规模模型（如 1.6B TabFM）相当的性能。

Method: Mitra-v2 采用小型 2D Transformer 骨干结构，预训练数据分布比 v1 更大更多样但仅使用合成数据；通过改进的优化策略使其能有效学习更大规模的任务分布；支持更长上下文和更大特征空间；模型参数规模约 77M。

Result: 在 TabArena 完整基准上达到 SOTA，与行业级 TabFM 和 EXAONE Tabular 持平，分类和回归任务均大幅超越 TabPFN-3。以 5% 的参数量匹配 1.6B TabFM。在 TALENT 基准上排名领先，明确优于 TabPFN-3 和 TabICLv2；在超过十个类别的分类任务上排名第一（尽管仅在最多十类的任务上预训练）。

Conclusion: Mitra-v2 是目前开源表格基础模型中最强大且应用最广泛的模型之一，在极小参数量下实现前沿性能，展示了仅用合成数据训练的模型能够达到乃至超越大规模真实数据训练模型的潜力。

Abstract: We introduce Mitra-v2, a tabular foundation model that delivers state-of-the-art performance on real-world classification and regression problems, from credit-risk scoring and clinical prediction to equipment-failure detection and house-price estimation. Mitra-v2 is trained only on synthetic data, with a pretraining distribution that is much larger and more diverse than Mitra-v1's. Built on a small 2D Transformer backbone, Mitra-v2 supports longer contexts and larger feature spaces. Improved optimization lets it learn from this larger task distribution. We evaluate Mitra-v2 on the TabArena and TALENT benchmarks, comprising more than 300 real-world datasets under two evaluation protocols. On the full TabArena benchmark, Mitra-v2 delivers state-of-the-art performance at the level of the industry-scale TabFM and EXAONE Tabular models, while surpassing TabPFN-3 by a wide margin in both classification and regression. Mitra-v2 matches the 1.6B-parameter TabFM with only 5% of its size (77M parameters), delivering frontier performance at a fraction of the cost. On TALENT, Mitra-v2 remains among the leading models, clearly outperforming TabPFN-3 and TabICLv2. It also ranks first on classification tasks with more than ten classes, even though it was pretrained only on tasks with at most ten classes. These results make Mitra-v2 one of the strongest and most broadly applicable open tabular foundation models released to date. We release the model weights, the inference and fine-tuning code, and our evaluation results under the Apache-2.0 license.

</details>


### [275] [Fast Surrogate Modeling of Excitable and Oscillatory FitzHugh-Nagumo Dynamics with Parametric Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04549)
*Andrew Franck,Justin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用参数条件化的傅里叶神经算子（FNO）作为FitzHugh-Nagumo（FHN）系统的快速可微分替代模型，通过FiLM调制将5D生理参数注入每个傅里叶层，分别在振荡（tonic firing）和可激发（action-potential propagation）两个区域训练算子，实现了亚0.1%的L2相对误差、近三个数量级的加速比，并准确复现了可激发分支结构（包括放电阈值和传导速度定律）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHN系统作为神经元电压动力学的简化模型，探索其5D生理参数空间对神经调控和将电压记录映射回生物物理至关重要，但经典有限差分求解器使得快速参数扫描代价高昂。因此需要开发一个既快速又可微分的替代模型来加速参数扫描。

Method: 在1D空间域上训练参数条件的FNO作为FHN电压场和恢复场的替代模型。每个傅里叶层通过特征级线性调制（FiLM）条件化于参数向量λ=(D_u, D_v, a, b, τ)。先通过分岔分析将模型覆盖的两个截然不同的区域——振荡（强直放电）和可激发（动作电位传播）——分开，然后在每个区域各训练一个算子。

Result: 在振荡区域，替代模型在两个场上均达到亚0.1%的相对L2误差，比有限差分基线运行快近三个数量级，在参数空间上均匀泛化，并在训练范围外仍保持低单位数百分比的预测误差。在可激发区域，同一算子准确复现了放电阈值和c ∝ √D_u传导速度定律，并完整复现行波脉冲，完全捕捉可激发分岔结构而非仅仅平滑插值场。

Conclusion: 参数条件的FNO能够在快速运行的同时准确捕捉FHN系统的两个不同动力学子区域及其物理/分岔结构，证明了神经算子作为生物物理参数扫描和可微分替代模型的有效性。

Abstract: The FitzHugh-Nagumo (FHN) system serves as a simplified model of neuronal voltage dynamics, capturing the activator-inhibitor structure behind both isolated action potentials and the rhythmic spiking seen across the brain. Exploring its 5D physiological parameter space is important for neuromodulation and mapping voltage recordings back to biophysics, yet classical finite-difference solvers make rapid parameter sweeps expensive. We train parameter-conditioned Fourier Neural Operators (FNOs) as fast, differentiable surrogates for the FHN voltage and recovery fields on a one-dimensional spatial domain, conditioning each Fourier layer on the parameter vector $λ= (D_u, D_v, a, b, τ)$ via feature-wise linear modulation (FiLM). We apply a single bifurcation analysis that delimits the two distinct regimes the model spans, oscillatory (tonic firing) and excitable (action-potential propagation), and we train one operator in each. In the oscillatory regime the surrogate attains sub-$0.1\%$ relative $L^2$ error on both fields, runs nearly three orders of magnitude faster than the finite-difference baseline, generalizes uniformly across the parameter space, and extrapolates to low single-digit percentage errors outside of the training bounds. In the excitable regime the same operator accurately reproduces the firing threshold and the $c \propto \sqrt{D_u}$ conduction-velocity law and replicates full traveling pulses, fully capturing the excitable bifurcation structure rather than just smoothly interpolating fields.

</details>


### [276] [Training-Free Halving of Activated Experts in Fine-Grained Mixture-of-Experts Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04575)
*Xing Chen,Hengshuai Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现细粒度MoE模型在推理时降低top-k激活专家数所导致的性能下降，主要源于重归一化引起的专家分支增益变化而非专家选择变化。通过引入一个无参数、无训练开销的整数k₂（用top k₁专家激活、但用top k₂的专家概率质量归一化），可大幅缓解性能下降，如Qwen3.6-35B-A3B从8降到4专家时MMLU仅损失0.35分（标准重归一化损失4.65分），且在大模型上同样复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MoE推理加速方法通过减少激活专家数来节省计算，但标准重归一化会将路由概率按top-k缩放，导致压缩时专家分支输出增益被错误放大，从而造成显著的性能损失。作者希望将"选择哪些专家"与"专家分支强度"这两个耦合效应解耦，以在不引入额外参数和训练的前提下实现更优的专家压缩。

Method: 提出一个极简方法：激活top k₁个专家，但使用top k₂个专家的概率质量进行归一化（仅需引入一个整数k₂）。当k₂固定为训练时的原始k值或更大值时，可保持专家分支的校准强度不变，从而隔离出纯专家选择的效应。作者在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B和Qwen3.5-397B-A17B两个尺度上系统评估了该方法。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上，从8到4专家时标准重归一化导致MMLU下降4.65分，而k₂=16时仅下降0.35分，同时路由计算减半。在11倍大的Qwen3.5-397B-A17B上，从10到5专家仅损失0.55分。完全移除重归一化是灾难性的。此外，困惑度和下游准确率偏好不同的k₂，且专家身份的重要性远大于专家权重。

Conclusion: MoE压缩时的性能损失主要由频率缩放/重归一化带来的专家输出增益失调引起，而非专家选择不当。通过合理设置参考概率质量k₂即可在不增加参数和训练成本的前提下实现接近无损的专家压缩。该工作还提醒研究者不应仅凭困惑度等无监督指标来筛选MoE压缩配置，因为其与下游准确率的最优点不一致。

Abstract: Modern fine-grained Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models route each token to a small number of experts and renormalize their router probabilities. We show that this renormalization implicitly calibrates expert output gain to the training top-$k$: reducing $k$ at inference changes not only which experts are used but also the strength of the expert branch. We separate these effects by activating the top $k_1$ experts while normalizing by the probability mass of the top $k_2$ experts, introducing one integer with no parameters, training, or measurable compute overhead. On Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, reducing from 8 to 4 experts causes a 4.65-point MMLU drop under standard renormalization but only 0.35 points with $k_2=16$, while halving routed-expert compute. The result replicates on the $11\times$ larger Qwen3.5-397B-A17B, where reducing from 10 to 5 experts loses only 0.55 points with an appropriate reference set. Removing renormalization entirely is catastrophic, showing that preserving a suitable reference mass is crucial. We further find that perplexity and downstream accuracy favor different $k_2$, cautioning against selecting MoE compression settings using unlabeled text alone. Analyses also show that expert identity matters substantially more than expert weighting, while balanced and domain-specialized routing leaves limited room for expert pruning.

</details>


### [277] [Optimizer Memory Schedules for Outscaling the Overtraining Axis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04577)
*Katie Everett,Shikai Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了优化器在过训练（overtraining）轴上的扩展行为，发现相对优化器性能和最优超参数随训练时长发生显著变化。在51M到253M参数模型、1x到256x过训练因子下，ADANA相比AdamW具有持久的扩展优势，而Muon和SOAP提供相对恒定的token效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往优化器评估通常在固定训练时长下进行，忽略了训练时长这一关键维度对优化器相对性能和最优超参数的影响。作者希望建立训练时长作为优化器评估与设计的基本轴，以期为实践者提供更全面的优化器选择指导。

Method: 对比四种优化器（AdamW、Muon、SOAP和ADANA），在51M至253M参数的模型上，跨越1x至256x过训练因子，并在每种设置下扫描基础学习率。分别考察矩阵预条件方法（Muon/SOAP）和动量调度方法（ADANA）相对AdamW的扩展行为，并额外对AdamW的固定记忆和ADANA的权重衰减与动量冷却进行单独调优。

Result: 发现：最优学习率调度可随过训练轴反转；最优权重衰减系数近似按sqrt(OT)扩展；更长训练时长偏好更长的固定记忆。ADANA的优势在单独调优AdamW固定记忆后仍然存在，且结合对数时间权重衰减和动量冷却后，其扩展指数优势接近DANA理论在幂律随机特征上的预测。Muon和SOAP则提供近似恒定的token效率优势，但SOAP在最高过训练因子下可能受益更多。ADANA开始时落后于矩阵预条件方法，但随训练推进反超Muon并与SOAP持平。

Conclusion: 训练时长是优化器评估与设计中不可忽视的核心轴。不同优化器在固定训练时长下与更长训练时长下的相对表现可能完全不同，且最优超参数（学习率调度、权重衰减、记忆长度）均随过训练因子系统性地变化。

Abstract: We investigate how optimizers scale across the overtraining axis and show that relative optimizer performance and optimal hyperparameters change substantially with training horizon. In particular, we study how matrix-preconditioned methods (Muon and SOAP) and a momentum-scheduled method (ADANA) scale relative to AdamW. We compare these four optimizers across models from 51M to 253M parameters and overtraining (OT) factors from 1x to 256x, sweeping the base learning rate at every setting. The preferred learning rate schedule can reverse across the overtraining axis, the best weight decay coefficient scales approximately as sqrt(OT), and longer horizons generally favor longer fixed memory. ADANA's scaling advantage over AdamW persists after tuning AdamW's fixed memory separately at each horizon. Log-time weight decay and momentum cooldown provide substantial gains for ADANA that compound as training increases. With this treatment, ADANA outscales AdamW with an exponent advantage close to that predicted by DANA theory on power-law random features. Muon and SOAP instead provide roughly constant token-efficiency advantages over AdamW across most of the measured range, although SOAP may gain further at the highest overtraining factors. ADANA begins behind both matrix-preconditioned optimizers but closes the gaps as training increases, surpassing Muon and becoming competitive with SOAP at our highest OT factors. These results establish training horizon as an essential axis for optimizer evaluation and design.

</details>


### [278] [Representation Redundancy and Structural Complexity in Finite-Field Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04583)
*Zheng Zhang,Na Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限域 F_{2^n} 上求逆运算在不同有序 F_2-基表示下的代数形式与学习难度变化，证明有序基与不同坐标求逆映射之间是 n 对一的关系（由 Galois 轨道决定），并分析了三种布尔公式化的代数程度和 ANF leap 差异，实验验证了理论结果及学习难度排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解数学运算的表示选择如何影响其代数形式和经验学习难度。特别是针对 F_{2^n} 上的求逆运算，在不同有序 F_2-基下的表现差异——这在密码学布尔函数设计中具有重要意义，因为求逆是许多密码原语（如AES S盒）的核心操作。

Method: （1）理论上证明两个有序基诱导相同的坐标求逆映射当且仅当它们属于同一 Galois 轨道；（2）分析三种布尔公式化：参考公式化（代数次数 n-1，联合 ANF leap 1）、混合表示公式化（次数 2(n-1)，联合 ANF leap 2）和完全原始公式化（次数至多 3(n-1)，联合 ANF leap 至少 n）；（3）通过穷举计算验证理论结果，并用多层感知机进行受控学习实验。

Result: 有序基与不同求逆映射之间精确为 n 对一对应关系。三种公式化的代数次数和 ANF leap 呈现递增趋势（从 n-1 到 3(n-1)），穷举实验与理论一致。MLP 实验显示学习难度与理论排序一致，但 Galois 轨道冗余在测试条件下仅提供有限的泛化收益。

Conclusion: 表示之间的精确冗余可以与布尔结构和学习行为的改变共存——当表示作为输入的一部分暴露时，即使表示在某种意义上是等价的（同一 Galois 轨道），其布尔公式化的复杂度和神经网络学习难度仍可能产生显著差异。

Abstract: The representation chosen for a mathematical operation can affect both its algebraic form and its empirical learning difficulty. We study this phenomenon for inversion over \(\mathbb F_{2^n}\), with field elements expressed in varying ordered \(\mathbb F_2\)-bases. We prove that two ordered bases induce the same coordinate inversion map if and only if they belong to the same Galois orbit. Since every orbit has size \(n\), the correspondence between ordered bases and distinct inversion maps is exactly \(n\)-to-one. We then analyze three Boolean formulations of inversion. The reference formulation has algebraic degree \(n-1\) and joint ANF leap \(1\), the mixed representation formulation has degree \(2(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap \(2\), and the complete raw formulation has degree at most \(3(n-1)\) and joint ANF leap at least \(n\). Exhaustive computations agree with the theoretical results and bounds in the cases considered. Controlled experiments with multilayer perceptrons show the same ordering in learning difficulty, while Galois orbit redundancy provides only a limited generalization benefit under the tested conditions. These results show that exact redundancy among representations can coexist with changes in Boolean structure and learning behavior when the representation is exposed as part of the input.

</details>


### [279] [GNN-Guided Graph Coarsening and Adaptive QUBO Penalties for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows on a Quantum Annealer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04593)
*Youssef Kamel Rezk,Paweł Gora*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究量子退火求解带时间窗的容量车辆路径问题（CVRPTW）时的图粗化方法。作者提出自适应惩罚校准方法（将平均原始约束违反从33.0降至0.06），并用图神经网络（GNN）替代人工调参的合并评分函数，在Solomon基准上实现跨家族统一配置（N=10时100%可行性，N=10-100时为83% vs 69%）。硬件实验（D-Wave Advantage2）验证了条件化效应在固定逻辑变量数下将可行样本从0.02%提升至39%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图粗化可将大规模QUBO问题缩小以便于量子退火求解，但现有粗化启发式算法需要针对特定家族调参且对随机实例不可靠。作者旨在解决这些局限性，实现无需家族特定调参的通用粗化方法。

Method: 1) 自适应惩罚校准：移除非绑定约束、归一化绑定约束并缩放剩余惩罚，控制内部系数范围以改善原始采样质量；2) 用图神经网络替代手工调优的合并评分函数，在Solomon所有家族上使用统一配置；3) 通过变量数保持控制实验区分条件化与问题规模的影响。

Result: 自适应惩罚校准将平均原始约束违反从33.0降至0.06（p=3.7e-11, n=56）。GNN粗化在N=10时所有Solomon家族均达100%可行性（R型为100% vs 启发式80%），N=10-100时可行性83% vs 69%，且85/90个实例-规模对上优于或持平。QUBO保持约5-6倍缩小。硬件实验中可行样本从0.02%增至39%。

Conclusion: 通过条件化改进（而非问题规模缩减）可显著提升量子退火求解CVRPTW的可行性；GNN粗化实现了跨家族统一的泛化性能；经典修复配合局部搜索仍是端到端求解成本的参考基线。

Abstract: Graph coarsening reduces the large Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) formulations arising when vehicle-routing problems are solved by quantum annealing. Nearby customers with compatible time windows are merged into super-nodes, the reduced problem is solved, and the solution is expanded to the original graph. For the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem with Time Windows (CVRPTW), existing coarsening heuristics require family-specific tuning and remain unreliable on random instances. We address these limitations on the Solomon benchmark using simulated annealing and a D-Wave Advantage2 processor.
  We first introduce adaptive penalty calibration. Uniform penalty scaling has little effect, whereas controlling the internal coefficient range substantially improves raw samples. Removing non-binding constraints, normalising binding ones, and scaling the remaining penalties reduces mean raw constraint violations from 33.0 to 0.06 at the same solver budget (p=3.7e-11, n=56). A variable-count-preserving control attributes this gain to conditioning rather than problem size.
  Second, we replace the hand-tuned merge score with a graph neural network (GNN) using one configuration across all families. At N=10, it achieves 100% feasibility across all Solomon families, including R-type (100% vs. 80% for the tuned heuristic). Across N=10,...,100, feasibility is 83% vs. 69%, with the GNN better or tied on 85/90 instance-size pairs. At N=80,100, the difference is significant (p=0.002; 25/25 pairs), while the QUBO remains approximately 5-6 times smaller.
  Finally, hardware experiments reproduce the conditioning effect at fixed logical variable count: feasible samples increase from 0.02% to 39% across 13 instances. Classical repair with local search remains a reference bound for end-to-end solution cost.

</details>


### [280] [Too Rare to Learn: Prescribed Cyclone Tracks Degrade a Bay of Bengal Ocean Emulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04635)
*Sumaiya Islam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文研究了孟加拉湾地区用于热带气旋暴露海域预报的神经海洋模拟器设计选择。作者比较了两个完全相同的U-Net模型，唯一区别是其中一个额外输入四个规定的气旋路径通道。基于GLORYS12再分析数据，论文扣留了15个完整气旋（强度65-150节）。结果显示，纯海洋模型在每种子的所有运行中都优于持续性预报，而气旋条件模型在所有运行中都输给持续性预报，两种技能范围完全不相交（p=3.1e-5）。原因是暴露频率而非信号内容：气旋通道仅在训练数据的7.9%天数上非零，因此一旦激活就会超出分布范围。额外误差落在规定的风暴足迹内，推理时用无风暴图替换真实气旋图可使持有气旋预报在每个种子上提升7.5%-16.4%。条件网络学会了针对罕见信号做出自信但错误的响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对气旋暴露沿海区域进行区域预报时，自然的设计思路是将气旋作为预设输入提供给神经网络。但该论文旨在验证这一设计选择是否真的有益，特别是在孟加拉湾这样的气旋多发海域。

Method: 从GLORYS12再分析中扣留15个强度65-150节的完整气旋，比较两个仅因额外四个规定气旋路径通道而不同的U-Net模型，使用三种随机种子进行实验，并通过将真实气旋图替换为无风暴图进行推理测试。

Result: 纯海洋模型在每次运行中都优于持续性预报，而气旋条件模型在每次运行中都输给它，两组技能范围完全不相交（p=3.1e-5）。推理时用无风暴图替换真实气旋图可在每个种子上提升持有气旋预报7.5%-16.4%。额外误差落在规定的风暴足迹内。

Conclusion: 将气旋作为预设输入的做法实际上是有害的。其根本原因是气旋通道在训练样本中暴露频率极低（仅7.9%天数非零），导致模型一旦激活这些通道就面临分布外问题，学习到了对罕见信号过度自信但错误的响应。

Abstract: Neural ocean emulators are being proposed for regional forecasting in cyclone-exposed coastal seas, and a natural design choice is to hand the network the cyclone as a prescribed input. We test that choice in the Bay of Bengal and find it harmful. We withhold 15 whole cyclones spanning 65 to 150 kt from GLORYS12 reanalysis and compare two U-Nets that are identical except for four prescribed cyclone-track channels. Across three seeds the ocean-only model beats persistence in every run and the storm-conditioned model loses to it in every run, with the two skill ranges disjoint (p = 3.1e-5, paired across storms). The cause is exposure frequency rather than signal content: the channels are non-zero on only 7.9% of training days, so they are out of distribution the moment they activate. The extra error falls inside the prescribed storm footprint, and replacing the real cyclone map with a no-storm map at inference improves held-out storm forecasts by 7.5 to 16.4% in every seed. The conditioned network has learned a response to a rare signal that is confidently wrong.

</details>


### [281] [SMILE: Bridging Continuous Optimization and Discrete Symbolic Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04639)
*Mansooreh Montazerin,Antonio Ortega,Ajitesh Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SMILE，一种混合符号回归框架，通过结构分析、连续优化和符号恢复三个阶段，统一了梯度优化与离散符号搜索，在SRBench基准上实现了最高的噪声鲁棒性和最优的精度-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号回归方法在组合搜索空间中收敛缓慢，且缺乏挖掘数据中组合结构（compositional structure）的机制，导致搜索效率低、鲁棒性差。

Method: SMILE包含三个阶段：(1) 结构分析——识别目标表达式的组合层次结构；(2) 连续优化——使用可解释激活函数（Sine、Multiplication、Identity、Logarithm、Exponential）学习编码目标表达式的网络参数；(3) 符号恢复——通过结构化剪枝、系数优化和取整，将学习到的网络蒸馏为包含精确符号常量的紧凑表达式。

Result: 在SRBench上，SMILE在最大噪声水平下取得了最高的符号求解率，展现出显著的鲁棒性；在精度-复杂度Pareto前沿上表现一致，能以远低于竞争方法的时间恢复更简单的表达式。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散优化策略能有效克服符号回归的组合搜索瓶颈，并利用数据中的组合结构实现更快、更稳健、更简洁的符号表达式发现。

Abstract: Symbolic regression (SR) discovers closed-form mathematical expressions from data, offering interpretability beyond black-box models. Existing methods suffer from slow convergence in combinatorial search spaces and lack mechanisms to exploit compositional structure in the data. We introduce SMILE (Sine, Multiplication, Identity, Logarithm, Exponential), a hybrid framework that unifies continuous gradient-based optimization with discrete symbolic recovery through three stages: structural analysis of the data to identify the compositional hierarchy of the target expression, continuous optimization to learn parameters of a network that encodes the target expression using interpretable activations, and symbolic recovery through structured pruning, coefficient optimization, and rounding. This final stage distills the learned network into a compact expression with exact symbolic constants. We evaluate SMILE on SRBench across ground-truth and black-box datasets, with ablation studies validating each component. SMILE achieves the highest symbolic solution rate at the largest noise levels, demonstrating strong robustness where competing methods degrade substantially. It consistently lies on the Pareto front of accuracy versus complexity, recovering significantly simpler expressions in a fraction of the time required by the competing methods.

</details>


### [282] [Interpretability for Turing Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04661)
*Billy Snikkers,Rumi Salazar,Daniel Murfet,Will Troiani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明敏感性（susceptibilities）这一神经网络可解释性技术能够通过探测嘈杂图灵机学习问题的局部损失景观，来识别图灵机中的算法结构。作者证明算法中的对称性和路径分离会在敏感性矩阵中诱导置换对称性和低秩块，并在确定性有限自动机（DFA）上验证了算法特征可通过主成分分析和聚类在敏感性空间中恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解神经网络内部是否编码了可解释的算法结构是AI可解释性的核心挑战。已有工作（Murfet和Troiani）提出了嘈杂图灵机的学习问题作为探测算法结构的研究框架，但尚不清楚现有的可解释性技术能否有效地在该设置中识别算法结构。

Method: 作者将敏感性（susceptibilities，一种衡量神经网络输入扰动对输出影响的局部损失景观分析技术）应用于Murfet和Troiani引入的嘈杂图灵机学习问题。理论上证明了图灵机算法的对称性在敏感性矩阵中表现为置换对称性，路径分离表现为低秩块结构。实验上在一组确定性有限自动机（DFAs）上验证这些理论结果，并使用主成分分析（PCA）和聚类方法在敏感性空间中恢复算法特征。

Result: 作者从理论上证明了对称性和路径分离分别对应于敏感性矩阵中的置换对称性和低秩块；在DFA数据集上的实验表明，算法特征能够通过PCA和聚类在敏感性空间中有效恢复。这些结果证实了敏感性作为探测学习系统中算法结构的工具的有效性。

Conclusion: 敏感性作为一种神经网络的局部损失景观探针，能够识别简单的算法结构（如DFA中的对称性和路径分离），为"网络内部是否真正实现了算法"这一根本问题提供了一种可计算的分析工具，弥合了可解释性与可计算性理论之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: We show that susceptibilities, an interpretability technique developed for neural networks, can identify the presence of algorithmic structure in Turing machines by probing the local loss landscape of a learning problem for noisy Turing machines introduced by Murfet and Troiani (arXiv:2504.08075). We prove that symmetries and path separation in the algorithm implemented by a Turing machine induce permutation symmetries and low-rank blocks in its susceptibility matrix. We study this empirically on a set of deterministic finite automata (DFAs) and demonstrate that algorithmic features can be recovered by principal component analysis and clustering methods in susceptibility space.

</details>


### [283] [WEECFP-SuRGE: Wide Embedded Extended Connectivity Fingerprint with Substructure Rotary Graph-distance Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04672)
*Robert Epps*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出WEECFP（无参数1024维连续分子指纹）与WEECFP-SuRGE（基于图距离旋转变换的Transformer架构）。WEECFP-SuRGE Blend在TDC ADMET榜单上平均回归排名最优，总排名第2（仅次于预训练的MapLight+GNN），在不使用外部预训练的方法中排名第1，并在Pgp、Lipophilicity等5项基准上取得榜首。在MoleculeNet的4项回归任务中击败3项的所有经典指纹基线。指纹重建可恢复99.9%的SMILES，图距离编码与真实距离Pearson r=0.901。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子表征方法在信息保留（指纹/子结构）与架构自适应性之间存在权衡：经典分子指纹（如Morgan）无法直接融入Transformer注意力，而图神经网络往往需要外部预训练才能达到最优性能。作者旨在提出一种无需外部预训练、无参数、近无损可重建且能有效编码图拓扑距离的分子指纹及配套注意力机制，以在ADMET与性质预测任务上超越现有基线。

Method: 1) WEECFP：将每个Morgan子结构散射到1024维向量中约32个带符号位置，形成无参数连续分子指纹。2) WEECFP-SuRGE：Transformer架构，自注意力采用SuRGE（基于分子最短路径图距离的RoPE-like旋转编码）作用于WEECFP子结构token。3) 集成7个模型形成Blend，无需外部预训练。4) 提出贪心重叠重建算法验证tokenization近无损性；提出三参考最远点图距离编码实现O(S)位置记忆。

Result: WEECFP-SuRGE Blend在TDC ADMET榜单：平均回归排名最低、总排名第2（仅次于预训练的MapLight+GNN）、在无外部预训练方法中排名第1；在Pgp、Lipophilicity、CYP2D6 Substrate、Clearance Microsome、LD50五项上取得榜首（WEECFP-NoSuRGE Blend另在HIA取得第1）。MoleculeNet上3/4回归任务（ESOL、Lipophilicity、QM9）超越所有经典指纹基线。贪心重建恢复99.9%分布内分子SMILES及98.93%跨数据集分子；图距离编码与真实距离Pearson r=0.901。

Conclusion: WEECFP及其SuRGE注意力架构证明了无需外部预训练即可在ADMET与分子性质预测任务上达到SOTA性能。子结构token化近无损、图距离旋转编码高效，表明该方法是分子Transformer建模的有力通用框架。

Abstract: We introduce WEECFP, a parameter-free 1024-dimensional continuous molecular fingerprint that scatters each Morgan substructure across roughly thirty-two signed positions of a single vector, and WEECFP-SuRGE, a transformer architecture whose self-attention applies SuRGE (Substructure Rotary Graph-distance Encoding) -- a RoPE-like rotation parameterized by molecular shortest-path graph distance -- to WEECFP substructure tokens. A 7-model blend of this architecture (the WEECFP-SuRGE Blend) achieves the lowest average regression rank on the TDC ADMET leaderboard; is #2 overall on the TDC ADMET leaderboard (behind only pretrained MapLight+GNN), and is #1 overall among methods that use no external pretraining; takes leaderboard #1 finishes on Pgp, Lipophilicity, CYP2D6 Substrate, Clearance Microsome, and LD50 (with the WEECFP-NoSuRGE Blend separately reaching #1 on HIA) across the full 22-benchmark suite -- without any external pretraining. On MoleculeNet, WEECFP-SuRGE beats every classical-fingerprint baseline on 3 of 4 regression tasks (ESOL, Lipophilicity, QM9). We further show that WEECFP tokenization is near-lossless: a greedy overlap reconstruction recovers the exact canonical SMILES of 99.9% of in-distribution molecules across 9 MoleculeNet datasets and 98.93% of molecules in a cross-dataset holdout (HIV->Lipophilicity), and that a three-reference farthest-first encoding of graph distance correlates at Pearson r = 0.901 with the true pairwise distance, enabling O(S) positional memory at matching accuracy.

</details>


### [284] [Simulation-free Unbalanced Dynamic Optimal Transport with General Growth Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04710)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SUDO（Simulation-free Unbalanced Dynamic Optimal Transport），一个无需仿真的非平衡动态最优传输框架，支持通用的非二次凸增长惩罚函数，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从非配对的单细胞快照中推断细胞动态需要同时建模状态转移与群体增殖/凋亡。UDOT通过沿传输路径惩罚增长来实现这一点，而增长惩罚的选择是编码增殖与凋亡生物学先验的关键途径。然而现有UDOT求解器要么依赖计算昂贵的NeuralODE模拟，要么受限于仅支持二次惩罚（即WFR测地线）的解析解，缺乏能高效处理通用增长惩罚的求解器。

Method: SUDO框架首先证明凹增长惩罚会导致退化解（生长与传输分离），从而聚焦于非二次凸增长惩罚。该框架学习条件路径与传输成本，求解诱导的半耦合问题，随后利用非平衡流匹配（unbalanced flow matching）实现无仿真求解。

Result: 在WFR基准上，SUDO匹配了高效解析解驱动算法的精度，同时在计算速度上超越基于仿真的方法。此外，SUDO支持编码增殖主导先验的非对称惩罚，在合成数据和单细胞数据集上产生更合理的轨迹与生长估计。

Conclusion: SUDO提供了一种高效且通用的UDOT求解方案，突破了此前仅支持二次惩罚（WFR）或依赖昂贵模拟的限制，支持更丰富的生物先验编码，在实际单细胞数据推断中产生更可靠的轨迹和生长估计。

Abstract: Inferring cellular dynamics from unpaired single-cell snapshots requires modeling both state transitions and population growth or death. Unbalanced dynamic optimal transport (UDOT) addresses this by penalizing growth along transport paths, making the choice of growth penalty a key way to encode biological priors on proliferation and apoptosis. However, existing UDOT solvers either rely on computationally expensive NeuralODE simulations or depend on analytical solutions of conditional paths, restricting their efficiency solely to quadratic penalties, i.e. Wasserstein-Fisher-Rao (WFR) geodesics. To enable an efficient UDOT solver for general growth penalties, we first show that concave growth penalties lead to degenerate solutions where growth and transport are separated. We then introduce \textbf{S}imulation-free \textbf{U}nbalanced \textbf{D}ynamic \textbf{O}ptimal transport (SUDO), a simulation-free framework for UDOT with general non-quadratic convex growth penalties. SUDO learns the conditional paths and transport costs, solves the induced semi-coupling problem, and subsequently leverages unbalanced flow matching to achieve a simulation-free solution. On WFR benchmarks, SUDO matches the accuracy of efficient, analytical solution-driven algorithms while outperforming simulation-based methods in computational speed. Beyond WFR, SUDO supports asymmetric penalties that encode proliferation-dominant priors and produce more plausible trajectories and growth estimates on synthetic and single-cell datasets.

</details>


### [285] [Locating and Steering Refusal Beyond Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04721)
*Preethi Carmel Bosco,Gopalakrishnan Srinivasan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究探讨推理拒答（refusal）能力在大语言模型与状态空间模型（SSM）等不同架构中的表示是否存在及是否可迁移。研究发现，拒答由残差流中的单一方向控制，该方向仅需一次刚性旋转即可跨架构对齐，证明不同架构共享同一安全表示。通过在该架构的"写入位点"（write site）重新估计方向，而非重建安全机制，拒答检测与防御可直接迁移到新架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全和可解释性工具严重依赖"拒答由Transformer残差流中单一方向控制"这一发现。然而状态空间模型通过循环更新而非注意力进行信息路由，与Transformer无共享的token混合机制。一个核心疑问是：这种安全表示是否能在架构差异下存续，还是必须针对每种架构重新发现。

Method: 作者对比了Transformer与SSM等不同架构，发现单一刚性旋转（只能重定向空间而无法重塑）即可将一个模型的表示空间与另一模型对齐，证明两者真正共享同一表示。他们训练一个基于Transformer的harm探针来检测SSM的有害输入，并验证移除对齐方向可使模型回答其本应拒绝的攻击。通过控制干预强度固定、仅改变方向估计位置的实验，区分了"方向在哪被引导"与"方向在何处被读取"的作用。最后在多架构（SSM、Transformer、循环、混合）上通过检测器触发的门控验证了该方向的防御效果，并排除了防御效果来自简单规则的可能性。

Result: 拒答方向在不同架构间确实共享：刚性旋转即可对齐表示空间，Transformer训练出的harm探针能识别SSM的有害输入，移除对齐方向显著降低拒答能力。关键发现是架构特异性不在于方向被引导到哪里，而在于它必须在哪被读取——每层的写入位点（输出在加入残差流之前）是可干净读取harm的位置。固定干预强度后，方向估计位置而非应用位置起决定作用。该方向在四种架构族中均能降低jailbreak成功率，且在SSM上能抵抗针对防御优化的攻击者。

Conclusion: 基于拒答的安全工具可以便捷地迁移到新架构：只需在新架构的写入位点重新估计方向，而无需重建安全机制。真正跨架构迁移的是方向本身而非防御强度，门控仅匹配简单拒答规则，说明迁移的核心价值在于共享的方向表示。

Abstract: Where inside a language model does refusal live, and does that place change when the architecture does? In a transformer, refusal is governed by a single direction in the residual stream, a finding that safety and interpretability tooling now depend on. State-space models (SSMs) route information through a recurrent update instead of attention, sharing no token-mixing mechanism with a transformer. Does the same safety representation survive this shift, or must it be rediscovered per architecture? It survives. A single rigid rotation, which can only reorient a space and not reshape it, aligns one model's representation space with another's, so the two genuinely share the representation. A harm probe trained on a transformer then flags an SSM's harmful inputs, and removing the aligned direction makes a model answer attacks it would otherwise refuse, while a random direction of the same size does far less. What is architecture-specific is not where the direction is steered but where it must be read. Each layer computes a fresh output that is then added into the residual stream, and harm is cleanly readable at this output, the write site, before the addition. A control that holds the intervention's strength fixed shows that what matters is where the direction is estimated, not where it is applied. Applied through a detector-triggered gate, this direction lowers jailbreak success in all four architecture families we test (SSM, transformer, recurrent, hybrid), and on the SSM it holds against an attacker that tunes its prompt against the defense. The gate only matches a trivial rule that returns a fixed refusal whenever the same detector fires, so what transfers across architectures is the direction itself, not defense strength. Safety tooling built on refusal therefore ports to a new architecture by re-estimating the direction at that architecture's write site, not by rebuilding it.

</details>


### [286] [Training Large Language Models for Small-Molecule Design with Synthetic Task Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04735)
*Frank Hu,Shriram Chennakesavalu,Zichen Wang,Patricia Suriana,Bodhi Vani,Kirill Shmilovich,Kangway Chuang,Colin Grambow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究是否可以让大型语言模型（LLMs）通过更便宜的合成任务学习分子设计策略，并将这些策略泛化到昂贵的分子先导化合物优化场景中。研究发现，基于课程学习的训练方案（逐步引入更具挑战性的合成设计任务）可以使模型在基于结构的先导化合物优化中超越更大规模的前沿模型，表明利用合成任务进行规模化后训练是将LLMs适配到高成本实验场景的有效策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计可行的药物候选分子需要在组合爆炸且崎岖的化学空间中搜索满足多个（通常相互竞争）目标的分子。LLMs因其表征能力、推理能力和从外部环境整合信息的灵活性，为这一问题提供了有用的生成先验。虽然基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）可以提升LLM能力，但许多化学相关的评分函数每次评估需要数小时甚至数天，直接用于在线训练成本过高。因此需要探索从廉价任务中学习、泛化到昂贵任务的方法。

Method: 本文提出使用课程式训练方案，逐步引入更具挑战性的合成设计任务来训练LLM进行分子设计。模型先在廉价的合成任务上通过RLVR训练，逐步过渡到更困难的任务，最终将习得的分子设计策略迁移到结构基的先导化合物优化（SBLOMO）这一高成本真实场景中。

Result: 课程式训练方案训练的模型在结构基先导化合物优化任务上表现出强劲性能，超越了更大规模的前沿模型（如frontier models）。这表明从合成任务中获得的能力可以有效迁移到昂贵的真实分子优化任务。

Conclusion: 利用合成任务进行规模化后训练是适应LLMs到高成本实验场景的有效策略——这些场景由于评估成本过高而无法直接进行在线RLVR训练。该工作为LLMs在药物设计中处理高成本实验反馈提供了可行的范式。

Abstract: Designing viable drug candidates requires searching a combinatorially large and rugged chemical space for molecules that satisfy multiple, often competing, objectives. Large language models (LLMs) provide a useful generative prior for this problem because of their representational capacity, reasoning ability, and flexibility when incorporating information from the external environment. While reinforcement learning from verifiable rewards (RLVR) can be used to improve the capabilities of LLMs, many chemically relevant scoring functions require hours or even days per evaluation, making them prohibitively expensive to use directly during online training. Here, we investigate whether LLMs can learn molecular design strategies from cheaper synthetic tasks that generalize to expensive molecular lead optimization settings. We find that curriculum-based training recipes that gradually incorporate more challenging synthetic design tasks enable strong performance that surpasses that of much larger frontier models on structure-based lead optimization. Our results suggest that scaling post-training using synthetic tasks is an effective strategy for adapting LLMs to high-cost experimental scenarios that are too expensive to directly train on.

</details>


### [287] [A Fairness Audit of the Duckworth-Lewis-Stern Method: Format-Specific and Gender-Differential Bias, with an Interpretable Calibration Layer for Cricket Target Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04754)
*Soumyadeep Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对DLS方法进行了迄今最大规模的实证审计（8,150场国际板球比赛、233,550个合成中断场景），发现DLS存在结构化偏差和性别差异偏差，并提出轻量级校准层DLS-Cal（27K参数）将绝对偏差降低31%（ODI）和19%（T20I），同时开源代码、模型和数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DLS方法自1999年以来一直是板球雨中断比赛目标修订的国际标准，使用超过二十年，但从未有大规模实证审计其预测偏差。作者旨在填补这一空白，量化DLS的系统性偏差并评估是否需要现代化替代方案。

Method: 从Cricsheet收集8,150场国际比赛（3,095场ODI和5,055场T20I），生成233,550个合成中断场景并采用时间切分。将DLS预测误差按（剩余回合数，已失 wickets）比赛状态分桶分析，检测结构化偏差和性别差异偏差。基准测试五种现代替代模型（Bi-LSTM、XGBoost、增强型XGBoost、深度上下文感知模型、堆叠集成），并提出轻量级可解释校准层DLS-Cal（27K参数），在DLS输出之上添加状态条件校正。

Result: DLS预测误差在不同比赛状态桶间跨越137分范围；在训练切分上，DLS对男子比赛平均过度预测+1.51分，对女子比赛过度预测+7.63分，性别差距达+6.13分（F=195.16, p<10^-43）。DLS-Cal将绝对偏差在ODI上降低31%、T20I上降低19%；性别感知变体将女子ODI残留偏差从+6.19分降至+0.65分，同时不影响男子校准。

Conclusion: DLS方法存在此前未被量化的系统性偏差，尤其对女子比赛偏差显著。DLS-Cal作为轻量级可解释的校准层，是保留DLS的成熟性与可解释性同时消除偏差的有效解决方案，且已开源全部资源以便复核与复现。

Abstract: The Duckworth-Lewis-Stern (DLS) method has been the international standard for revising target scores in rain-interrupted limited-overs cricket since 1999. Despite over two decades of operational use, no large-scale empirical audit of its prediction bias has been published. We conduct such an audit on 8,150 international matches (3,095 ODIs, 5,055 T20Is) from Cricsheet, generating 233,550 synthetic interruption scenarios with temporal splits. We document two structured biases. First, DLS prediction error spans a 137-run range across (overs-remaining, wickets-lost) match-state buckets. Second, DLS exhibits a gender-differential bias on ODIs that has not previously been quantified: on the training split, mean over-prediction is +1.51 runs for men but +7.63 runs for women, a gap of +6.13 runs (F = 195.16, p < 10^-43). We benchmark DLS against five modern alternatives: Bi-LSTM, XGBoost, an enriched XGBoost variant, a deep context-aware model, and a stacking ensemble, and propose DLS-Cal, a lightweight interpretable calibration layer (27K parameters) outputting a state-conditioned correction added to DLS. DLS-Cal reduces absolute bias by 31% on ODI and 19% on T20I, and a gender-aware variant reduces women's ODI residual bias from +6.19 to +0.65 runs while leaving men's calibration unchanged. We release code, models, and data.

</details>


### [288] [Resilience Beyond Stationary Client Unavailability: Unlocking Efficient and Unbiased Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04763)
*Ming Xiang,Stratis Ioannidis,Edmund Yeh,Carlee Joe-Wong,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedSWE，一种面向异构且非平稳随机客户端可用性的联邦学习算法，通过补偿缺失计算、稳定全局更新及隐式高斯混合机制，在仅有轻微额外开销下证明其收敛到非凸目标的驻点并具备线性加速性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实联邦学习系统中客户端常因资源限制或外部不确定性而间歇性不可用，且服务器缺乏对客户端可用性的实时先验知识。传统算法对客户端可变性缺乏鲁棒性，易引入偏差损害模型性能。现有工作要么忽略非平稳可用性动态，要么需要大量内存和计算开销。

Method: 提出FedSWE算法，具有三方面新颖算法结构：(i)补偿错过的计算；(ii)跨轮次稳定并扩散全局更新；(iii)通过隐式gossiping均匀混合局部更新。该算法不依赖非平稳动态的先验知识，相比FedAvg仅引入轻微额外内存和计算开销。

Result: 理论证明FedSWE在非凸目标下收敛到驻点，并在特定情况下实现期望的线性加速性质；通过真实数据集上多样化的客户端不可用动态数值实验验证了分析结果。

Conclusion: FedSWE在异构且非平稳随机客户端可用性下具有可证明的鲁棒性，兼具低开销和良好收敛保证，为现实联邦学习中的不可靠客户端场景提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Due to resource constraints or external and internal uncertainties, clients in real-world federated learning systems are often intermittently available edge devices. In highly dynamic environments, the parameter server lacks prior real-time knowledge of clients' availability, making it challenging to adapt traditional federated learning algorithms to be resilient to uncertainties in client availability. If not carefully addressed, complex client availability can introduce significant bias, potentially harming the performance of the trained model. Most prior work either fails to account for non-stationary client availability dynamics or demands significant memory and computational overhead. This paper aims to develop efficient federated learning algorithms that are provably resilient to heterogeneous and non-stationary stochastic client availability. We propose FedSWE, which admits novel algorithmic structures to (i) compensate for missed computations, (ii) stabilize and diffuse the global updates over rounds, and (iii) evenly mix the local updates through implicit gossiping, despite being agnostic to non-stationary dynamics. Compared with the standard FedAvg, FedSWE introduces light additional memory and computation overhead. We show that FedSWE converges to a stationary point of non-convex objectives while achieving the desired linear speedup property in certain special cases. We corroborate our analysis with numerical experiments over diversified client unavailability dynamics on real-world data sets.

</details>


### [289] [A Robust Watermark-based Fingerprint Framework for GNNs Ownership Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04772)
*Han Zhang,Yan Wang,Guanfeng Liu,Pengfei Ding,Huaxiong Wang,Kwok-Yan Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出REMARK框架，通过生成分布内水印图并从中提取鲁棒指纹来验证GNN模型所有权，克服了现有水印和指纹方法的三大局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GNN训练成本高昂，模型所有权被侵犯（如模型窃取和未授权使用）导致严重经济损失。现有GNN所有权验证方法存在三个问题：(1)分布外水印图导致受保护模型性能下降；(2)假设替代模型在水印数据集上训练，现实中不成立；(3)指纹提取过度依赖特定输出层。

Method: REMARK首先精心生成分布内（in-distribution）水印图，最大化GNN模型间的输出差异，从而缓解分布外数据引起的性能退化；随后从这些输出差异中提取鲁棒指纹用于GNN所有权验证，消除了替代模型须在水印数据集上训练或暴露特定输出层的假设。

Result: 在广泛使用的真实数据集和多种GNN架构上的大量实验表明，REMARK在所有权验证准确率和鲁棒性方面达到最先进水平，同时保持了受保护模型的效用。

Conclusion: REMARK通过结合水印与指纹方法，有效克服了现有GNN所有权验证方法的关键缺陷，实现了高准确率、强鲁棒性且不损害模型性能的所有权验证方案。

Abstract: The high training cost of Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) has raised growing concerns regarding model ownership infringement, such as model stealing and unauthorized misuse. To verify model ownership and prevent significant economic losses, two groups of GNN Ownership Verification (OV) methods have been proposed: watermark-based methods and fingerprint-based methods. However, these methods typically face three limitations: (1) the performance degradation of protected models caused by out-of-distribution (OOD) watermark graphs with respect to the training set; (2) the unrealistic assumption that surrogate models have been trained on a watermark-containing training set; and (3) over-reliance on specific output levels for fingerprint extraction. In this paper, we propose a Robust watErMArk-based fingeRprint frameworK for GNNs, named REMARK. REMARK first generates carefully crafted in-distribution watermark graphs that maximize output differences between GNN models, thus mitigating OOD-induced performance degradation. REMARK then extracts robust fingerprints from these output differences to verify GNN ownership, thereby removing the assumptions that surrogate models must be trained on a watermark-containing dataset or expose specific output levels. Extensive experiments across widely used real-world datasets and GNN architectures demonstrate that REMARK achieves state-of-the-art OV accuracy and robustness while preserving the utility of protected models.

</details>


### [290] [Persistent Teacher Anchoring for Tool-Using Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04773)
*Hyun Bin Park,Kyungho Song,Sangmin Lee,Du-Seong Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出持久教师锚定（PTA）方法，用于解决LLM后训练中基于策略的知识蒸馏（OPKD）在工具使用场景下师生分布漂移的问题。PTA在分块验证基础上增加回合级承诺，确保教师验证完整回合后才允许工具调用执行，并引入持久前瞻（persistent lookahead）机制以利用空闲rollout容量。在检索和感知强化学习任务中，PTA相对OPKD在最佳@4指标上提升2.5和2.8个点，吞吐量提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM后训练中，ON-policy知识蒸馏让学生在每个状态匹配教师输出的下一token分布。当学生rollout进入教师不会访问的状态时，师生分布差距会累积。在工具使用场景中，学生编写的函数调用在监督之前就执行，其观察结果影响后续前缀，使这一差距问题变得严重。现有proposer-verifier生成框架只覆盖文本生成，未涵盖工具执行，需要更系统的解决方案。

Method: PTA是一种学生诱导但教师承诺的rollout构造方法。具体包括：(1)保留分块级验证，增加回合级承诺（turn-level commitment），使工具调用只有在教师验证完整个回合后才能到达环境；(2)将已验证的分块视为原子生成单元，引入持久前瞻（persistent lookahead），利用空闲rollout容量推进未来样本，并在固定验证器下跨学生更新携带未完成的rollout。

Result: 在Search-R1风格的检索RL和DeepEyes风格的感知RL任务上，在相同下游RL预算下，PTA相比OPKD在宏观best@4指标上分别提升2.5和2.8个点；持久前瞻机制将吞吐量提升24%。

Conclusion: PTA通过回合级承诺弥补了现有proposer-verifier方法在工具执行监督上的空白，有效缓解了工具使用场景下师生分布漂移问题，并在下游RL训练中带来一致的性能提升和吞吐量改善。

Abstract: Distillation is common in LLM post-training, where on-policy knowledge distillation (OPKD) uses student-generated trajectories to prepare the student for downstream RL. At each state, the student matches a next-token distribution supplied by the teacher. As the rollout enters states the teacher would not visit, the teacher-student distribution gap can accumulate. In tool use, this gap becomes consequential because student-written calls execute before supervision and their observations shape later prefixes. Proposer-verifier generation addresses this drift by letting the teacher decide which student-proposed text is retained during generation. Existing formulations govern text but leave tool execution outside their scope. We propose Persistent Teacher Anchoring (PTA), a student-induced but teacher-committed rollout construction. PTA retains chunk-level verification and adds turn-level commitment, allowing a call to reach the environment only after the teacher has verified the entire turn. Treating verified chunks as atomic generation units, we introduce persistent lookahead, which fills idle rollout capacity by advancing future samples and carrying unfinished ones across student updates under the fixed verifier. Across Search-R1-style retrieval and DeepEyes-style perception RL, applying PTA before downstream RL improves macro best@4 by 2.5 and 2.8 points over OPKD under the same downstream RL budget, while lookahead improves throughput by 24%.

</details>


### [291] [Dynamic Heterogeneous Graph Representation Learning: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04779)
*Huan Liu,Pengfei Jiao,Jie Yin,Hongjiang Chen,Zhidong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是首个系统综述动态异质图（DHG）表示学习方法的论文，提出了统一的形式化定义、算法中心分类法，涵盖嵌入方法、GNN方法和Transformer方法，并总结了应用、数据集及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的AI系统本质上是不断演化的异质实体及其复杂交互，静态或同质图建模难以应对这些复杂性。因此需要系统梳理DHG表示学习方法，以同时保留结构语义和时间动态。

Method: 论文首先从时间粒度视角提出涵盖离散时间和连续时间DHG的统一形式化定义；然后提出以算法为中心的新分类法，将现有文献分为早期基于嵌入的方法、基于GNN的模型和较新的基于Transformer的DHG方法，并明确其时间粒度上的内在建模偏差；最后总结了代表性应用、常用数据集和基准。

Result: 论文提供了一个全面的DHG表示学习综述框架，包含统一的形式化定义、算法中心分类学（嵌入/图神经网络/Transformer三类），以及对监督学习相关的应用、数据集和基准的汇总。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了未来有前景的研究方向，为动态异质图表示学习这一快速发展的领域提供指导。该工作填补了该方向缺乏系统性综述的空白。

Abstract: Graph representation learning (GRL) serves as a canonical paradigm for modeling complex networks. However, real-world AI systems inherently manifest as evolving heterogeneous entities with complex interactions, posing significant challenges to static or homogeneous modeling. To address these complexities, representation learning for Dynamic Heterogeneous Graphs (DHGs) has emerged as a vital approach for learning low-dimensional representations that simultaneously preserve structural semantics and temporal dynamics. This survey presents the first systematic review of DHG representation learning methods. We first introduce a unified formal definition that encompasses both discrete-time and continuous-time DHGs from the perspective of temporal granularity. Building upon this formulation, we propose a novel algorithm-centric taxonomy that categorizes existing literature, including early embedding-based approaches, graph neural network (GNN)-based models, and relatively recent Transformer-based DHG methods, while explicitly highlighting their intrinsic modeling biases with respect to dynamic granularity. Furthermore, we summarize representative applications of DHG representation learning, along with commonly used datasets and benchmarks. Finally, we discuss promising research directions that guide future advances in this rapidly evolving field.

</details>


### [292] [Learning-Augmented Algorithms: Guarantees, Construction Mechanisms, and System-Level Implications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04787)
*Hailiang Zhao,Peng Chen,Xueyan Tang,Jianwei Yin,Shuiguang Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于学习增强算法（learning-augmented algorithms）的综述，系统综合了预测接口、误差度量、一致性-鲁棒性权衡以及五种代表性构造机制，覆盖在线优化、缓存、学习数据结构、图问题和机制设计等多个领域，并对理论保证与实证证据进行了明确区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习增强算法利用可能出错的预测同时保留形式化性能保证，但该领域缺乏一个统一的视角来整合预测接口设计、误差度量和构造机制等分散的研究成果。作者旨在通过跨领域综合，梳理其中的通用原则并明确开放问题。

Method: 该综述采用正交的定理级分析视角，区分了可达上界与匹配的渐近依赖性；将形式化保证与经验系统证据分离，并显式处理预测成本、反馈机制和组合性；综合了五类代表性构造机制，并提出了有限端到端推理的充分条件。

Result: 作者提出了一个跨领域的综合框架，区分预测接口、误差度量与一致性-鲁棒性权衡；同时梳理了理论结果的渐近特征，并划定了预测成本感知、内生误差、语义预测器和基准测试等方向的开放问题。

Conclusion: 学习增强算法领域需要进一步关注代价感知的预测、内生误差建模、语义层次的预测器设计以及标准化基准测试等方向，以实现从单点理论保证走向有限端到端推理的闭环。

Abstract: Learning-augmented algorithms use fallible predictions while retaining formal performance guarantees. This survey synthesizes prediction interfaces, error measures, consistency--robustness trade-offs, and five representative construction mechanisms across online optimization, caching, learned data structures, graph problems, and mechanism design. An orthogonal theorem-level axis distinguishes achieved upper bounds from matched asymptotic dependence. Formal guarantees are separated from empirical systems evidence, with explicit treatment of prediction cost, feedback, and composition. The resulting synthesis states sufficient conditions for limited end-to-end reasoning and delineates open problems in cost-aware prediction, endogenous error, semantic predictors, and benchmarking.

</details>


### [293] [How Faithful Is Attribution for Sales Forecasting? A Counterfactual Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04797)
*Glib Kechyn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为深度销售预测模型（WaveNet）添加了一个事后、与架构无关的反事实可解释性层，将其应用于Corporacion Favorita杂货数据集（174,685个序列、1,688天）。该方法将每个预测分解为精确加总等于预测值的贡献，避免加法SHAP式归因的分配伪影。通过删除/插入协议进行忠实度评估，两种检验均达到统计显著性（删除差距0.22, p<0.001；插入差距0.27, p<0.01，且在五个背景采样种子下稳健），证明归因反映了真实模型行为。文章诚实地刻画了归因的适用边界：促销信号的依赖在不同序列间具异质性，模型能捕捉每周销售周期形状（工作日r=0.78）但系统性低估其振幅。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度销售预测模型（如WaveNet式膨胀卷积网络）虽然准确但缺乏可解释性：当单一模型为众多时间序列之一预测销售时，它无法解释"为什么"给出该预测。作者旨在为这类多序列预测模型提供一种可解释性方法，且该方法必须能够忠实反映模型真实行为而非仅产生看似合理的伪影。

Method: 提出一种事后、与架构无关的反事实可解释性层。与加法SHAP式归因不同，该方法将每个预测分解为若干贡献项，且这些贡献精确加总等于预测值，从而避免分配伪影。随后通过删除/插入协议评估归因的忠实度，并在多个背景采样种子下检验稳健性。

Result: 忠实度检验在删除和插入两种协议下均显著（删除差距0.22, p<0.001；插入差距0.27, p<0.01），在五个背景采样种子下稳健。归因的语义分析显示：促销信号依赖在序列间具高度异质性（中位比值约1.0，约20%序列表现出强效应）；模型能捕捉每周销售周期的形状（按日相关系数r=0.78），但系统性低估其振幅。

Conclusion: 本工作的贡献不在于提升预测精度，而在于提供一个具有严格忠实度评估的反事实可解释性层，并对其适用边界和局限性给出坦诚的说明——既证明了归因反映真实模型行为，也明确指出归因在哪些场景下信息量有限。

Abstract: Deep models for sales forecasting, such as WaveNet-style dilated convolutional networks, are accurate but opaque: when a single model predicts sales for one of many series, it offers no account of why. We add a post-hoc, architecture-agnostic counterfactual interpretability layer to a multi-series WaveNet forecaster trained on the full Corporacion Favorita grocery dataset (174,685 series over 1,688 days). The method decomposes each forecast into contributions that sum exactly to the predicted value, avoiding the allocation artifacts we observed with additive SHAP-style attribution. We evaluate faithfulness with a deletion/insertion protocol and find a statistically significant effect on both tests (deletion gap 0.22, p<0.001; insertion gap 0.27, p<0.01; robust across five background-sampling seeds), establishing that the attributions reflect genuine model behavior rather than plausible-looking artifacts. We then characterize, honestly, where attribution is and is not informative: reliance on the promotion signal is heterogeneous across series (median ratio approximately 1.0, with roughly 20% of series showing a strong effect), and the model captures the shape of the weekly sales cycle (day-of-week r=0.78) while systematically under-predicting its amplitude. Our contribution is not improved accuracy but an interpretability layer with a rigorous faithfulness evaluation and a candid account of its limits.

</details>


### [294] [Federated Attack Campaign Detection via Contrastive Encoding of Threat Indicators in Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04815)
*Manuel Röder,Bibin Babu,Frank-Michael Schleif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FedIoC，一个模块化的联邦学习框架，用于在不共享敏感威胁情报的情况下检测跨组织的协同网络攻击活动。客户端使用基于IoC匹配流的监督对比学习将本地结构化威胁指标编码进梯度更新中，服务器通过聚类客户端梯度的余弦相似度来恢复全球攻击活动模式，无需直接传输IoCs。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测跨多个组织的协同网络攻击活动通常需要共享敏感遥测数据和威胁情报，这涉及机构和国家边界，存在隐私与合规障碍。联邦学习虽可解决此问题（直接在本地数据上训练共享检测器），但如何让联邦学习在客户端本地碎片化观察条件下恢复全局攻击活动模式仍是一个开放挑战。

Method: FedIoC采用模块化框架：(1)客户端侧编码器使用监督对比损失，在每批训练中，匹配已知IoC模式的流量构成正样本集，对比目标拉近其嵌入并推开非IoC嵌入，使活动相关结构显式体现在梯度方向中；(2)共享同一攻击活动指标的客户端产生对齐的梯度分量，服务器通过余弦相似度对这些更新进行聚类，恢复全局活动模式而无需直接的IoC传输。

Result: 在两个公共威胁检测基准上进行了评估，各客户端仅观察到每个活跃活动的片段并持有基于本地遥测的互不相交的指标集。在此设置下，FL服务器能够直接从梯度几何结构中恢复跨组织的活动群组。研究还指出非IID梯度结构是活动恢复的主要驱动力。

Conclusion: FedIoC作为该场景下的模块化框架被提出，并用来精确定位非IID梯度结构是威胁活动恢复的主要驱动因素，同时定义了设计改进型编码器的开放问题，为未来研究指明方向。

Abstract: Detecting orchestrated cyberattack campaigns that span multiple organizations traditionally requires sharing sensitive telemetry and threat intelligence across institutional boundaries and country borders, a barrier that Federated Learning removes by training shared threat detectors directly on local data. We propose FedIoC, a modular framework in which clients fold locally available structured threat indicators into their gradient updates; we instantiate the client-side encoder with a supervised contrastive loss over IoC-matched flows. Within each training batch, flows that match any known indicator pattern form the positive set; the contrastive objective pulls their learned embeddings together and pushes non-IoC embeddings away, so that campaign-relevant structure is, by design, expressed in the gradient direction. Clients sharing indicators for the same attack campaign then produce aligned gradient components, which the server clusters by the cosine similarity of their updates to recover global campaign patterns without any direct IoC transmission. We evaluate FedIoC on two public threat-detection benchmarks distributed across FL clients that each observe only a fragment of every active campaign and hold disjoint indicator sets derived from their local telemetry. In this regime the FL server recovers cross-organizational campaign cohorts directly from gradient geometry. We contribute FedIoC as a modular framework for this setting, and use it to pinpoint the non-IID gradient structure as the main driver of recovery and to define the open problem of designing encoders that improve on it.

</details>


### [295] [Communication-Efficient Personalized Federated Learning via Layer-Wise Multi-Threshold Random Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04830)
*Xu Zhang,Xingyu Hou,Jiacheng Cheng,Kaiyuan Feng,Maoguo Gong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于逐层多阈值随机草图的通信高效个性化联邦学习框架，通过为每一层分配独立的量化阈值集合并使用多区间进行精细低比特描述，解决现有单阈值一位压缩方法忽视层间参数分布差异和量化敏感性的问题，支持双向通信并改善通信-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化联邦学习虽能解决数据异构性，但在带宽受限系统中交换高维参数会产生巨大的上下行通信开销。现有的一位压缩方法采用单一阈值规则应用于整个模型，存在两个局限：一是忽视了不同层之间参数分布和量化敏感性的差异；二是单一阈值仅提供粗糙的二元信息，无法捕捉参数分布的细微变化。

Method: 提出层-wise多阈值随机草图（layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching）框架。每一层分配独立的量化阈值集合，使压缩表示能够适应层特定统计特性，同时利用多个间隔对被草图的参数提供更精细的低比特描述。该方法使用紧凑低比特草图以支持双向通信。

Result: 与现有的一位压缩方法相比，所提出的方法改善了通信-精度权衡并支持上行和下行的双向通信。

Conclusion: 通过逐层自适应多阈值量化设计，框架在保持个性化联邦学习效益的同时显著降低通信成本，为带宽受限的分布式学习环境提供了一种比单一阈值一位压缩更优的方案。

Abstract: Personalized federated learning (PFL) is a promising paradigm for collaborative learning over distributed devices, where edge nodes collaboratively train personalized models without sharing raw data. Although PFL addresses data heterogeneity by learning client-specific models, it still suffers from substantial uplink and downlink communication costs when exchanging high-dimensional parameters in bandwidth-constrained systems. Recent one-bit methods achieve extreme compression, but they usually rely on a single thresholding rule applied to the whole model. This design has two limitations. First, it overlooks layer-wise differences in parameter distributions and quantization sensitivities. Second, a single threshold provides only coarse binary information and cannot capture fine-grained variations in parameter distributions. To address these issues, we propose a communication-efficient PFL framework via layer-wise multi-threshold random sketching. In the proposed method, each layer is assigned its own set of quantization thresholds, so that the compressed representation can adapt to layer-specific statistics while using multiple intervals to provide a finer low-bit description of sketched parameters. The proposed method supports bidirectional communication using compact low-bit sketches and improves the communication-accuracy tradeoff compared with existing one-bit compression approaches.

</details>


### [296] [PACE: Propagation-Aware Collaborative Correction for One-Shot Personalized Federated Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04832)
*Ruizhe Huang,Chengran Li,Xiaochuan Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出PACE框架，在一次性通信的个性化联邦图学习中，将协作知识作为对客户端本地模型的紧凑"校正"而非替代品传输。通过上传秩-r更新载体和对角消息矩草图，服务器构建传播感知的接收者锚定校正；接收端用凸负对数似然校准（CNLL）在本地和外部logits间选择系数。实验显示PACE能在五个数据集上提升精度和加权F1，在ogbn-arxiv上则完全保留本地预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦图学习中客户端异质性既是机会也是风险：其他子图知识可能补充本地模型，但不兼容的迁移会覆盖可靠预测。一次性通信加剧了这一矛盾，因为不合适的服务端返回无法事后修正。现有方法将外部知识作为本地模型的替代品引入，缺乏对兼容性的保护。

Method: PACE的核心设计包括：(1) 每个客户端上传秩-r更新载体和对角传播消息矩草图；(2) 服务端构建传播感知的接收者锚定校正（receiver-anchored correction）；(3) 接收端保留完整本地模型，通过凸负对数似然校准（CNLL）在验证节点上为本地logits与外部logits选择一个混合系数；(4) 模型参数保持固定，无需反馈往返。

Result: 在秩为6时，个性化返回仅占用六个数据集稠密张量字节的9.6-17.6%。校正获得非零权重并在五个数据集上同时提升Accuracy和加权F1；在ogbn-arxiv上CNLL赋予校正零预测权重，精确保持本地预测。对三个引文数据集上的匹配基线应用相同CNLL规则无法解释这些增益。

Conclusion: 核心结论是：一个小的传输校正能够在接收端证据支持时增强完整本地模型，而在不支持时保持本地预测不变。这为一次性个性化联邦图学习提供了一种既能利用协作知识又能防止异质性损伤的机制。

Abstract: Client heterogeneity creates both an opportunity and a risk in personalized federated graph learning. Knowledge held by other subgraphs may complement a receiver's Local model, but an incompatible transfer can override reliable predictions.
